- **curaye-cohere**: Run a coherence pass on a Curaye-managed project — find overlapping features, inconsistent UX and UI, and duplicated or divergent implementation patterns that accumulate when an app is built spec by spec. - **curaye-preview**: Preview how a Curaye spec will be built before building it — screens and states for UI work, a sample terminal session for CLI work, signatures for libraries, before/after layouts for data and refactors — then store the approved approach with the spec so it can be compared with what lands. Use before /curaye-build on any non-trivial spec; /curaye-build also runs it automatically for effort m and up or UI work. Pass a spec id. - **curaye-ship**: Graduate a completed Curaye spec from planned/ to shipped/, update the current/ layer, and optionally sync stack.md and CLAUDE.md.
